Manus switches viewer by tool kind and keeps a picture-in-picture mini view that follows the user [SRC]. Codex's exec_command takes tty, yield time and max output tokens and returns a session id with partial output for write_stdin polling [SRC]. Cursor stores each terminal as a file and offloads big outputs to disk [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** ChatLiveRunPanel mounts only BrowserChrome and a screenshot rail (apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/src/features/chat/components/ChatLiveRunPanel.tsx:590-625), but a ToolIntent registry already renders terminal, search, read and diff presentations (apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/src/shared/chat-nodes/tool-presentation.ts:1-80). The process_* family exists with a redacting batcher, durable registry and cursor pull (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/execution-core/src/process_tools.rs:1-70), but uses pipe stdio, needs a Space lease and a feature flag, and has no human read path for output. Key the viewer registry on ToolIntent with browser, process and file kinds; add an optional tty flag to process_start and reuse process_read instead of a parallel exec contract; add output spill in the same family. The unbuilt human output read path is a prerequisite for the terminal viewer.

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-009](#F-t3-009), [F-t3-008](#F-t3-008); desktop viewer blocked on [F-t3-024](#F-t3-024); registry output must fall under ZDR and erasure; no interactive user shell.

<a id="F-t3-036"></a>
### F-t3-036 · Wide research: user entry point, honest estimate and spend gate, per-item retry, replay after reload
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: L · Plane: Model Plane · Wave: W3 · Confidence: High*

**What and why.** Give the existing research fan-out engine a product surface: a "Bred forskning" control with item-count presets, a start card stating items, rounds and minutes, a per-item grid with retry, a mid-run spend check, and a grid that survives reload. The engine is already paid for and serves Verevon's B2B list work (suppliers, competitors, leads).

**Reference practice.** Manus is the only source, 2 of 12 references (both Manus surfaces), so this is a differentiator [SRC][DOC]. A controller splits the task into independent items, shows a cost range and a start card, spawns up to K isolated sub-runs, streams per-item status into a grid with retry, and after the first N items offers Continue or Wrap up before merging; sub-agents never talk to each other [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** The engine runs N delegated Researcher children behind a semaphore (default 4, cap 16) at `apps/Model Plane/rust/services/execution-core/src/subagent/research_fan_out.rs:1-110`, and ChatWideResearchPanel shows live cards by batchId (`apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/src/features/chat/components/ChatWideResearchPanel.tsx:1-130`). Missing: composer entry point, estimate card, spend check, per-item retry and replay; execution-core has no cost-core client. Plan rows C3 to C10 and OD7 already own entry point, limits, replay and credentials; the delta here is the estimate card (RunDeadline in `run_budget.rs:1-30` plus observed per-item time, USD only on metered routes), the spend check, and retry that re-spawns one child with the full item text. Rebuild the grid from lineage edges; under Zero-Data-Retention (ZDR) it stays live-only. Do not use the Go WideResearchWorkflow: it hardcodes run ids and cannot fire from a scoped schedule (`workflow_dispatcher.go:215-260`).

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-028](#F-t3-028), [F-t3-002](#F-t3-002), OD7; the org cap must clamp client-requested counts server-side, and the 300 s delegated bearer bounds scale until C9 or OD8.

<a id="F-t3-037"></a>
### F-t3-037 · Persistent goals with a token budget, a completion judge and auto-continuation
*Verevon today: Absent · Effort: XL · Plane: Model Plane · Wave: W3 · Confidence: High*

**What and why.** Add a durable Goal record {objective, success_criteria, token_budget, used, status} and a continuation driver that starts budgeted Do runs until an audited judge confirms the criteria or a budget or blocker stops it. Today a user cannot hand over a multi-step objective and come back to a result.

**Reference practice.** Goals appear in 6 of 12 references [SRC][DOC]. Codex persists a ThreadGoal with a token budget and an audited completion check; Hermes adds an auxiliary judge with quality gates; the DeepSeek harness shows a goal bar with compare-and-set edits [SRC]. When the run goes idle, the driver re-prompts with the goal state and a cheap judge checks completion with evidence; the worker never self-declares done [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** No Goal record, budget ledger, judge or driver exists. The nearest primitives are an in-memory TeamWorker without budget or judge (`apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/coordinator.rs:1-80`) and the scheduled-step lane, one tool-less 1,024-token inference per step (`apps/Model Plane/rust/services/execution-core/src/grpc.rs:496-525`), which cannot continue a Do run. Build (1) a Goal record and ledger in session-core, non-ZDR only; (2) a continuation workflow modelled on ScheduledRunSupervision that takes fresh Control authorization per continuation and starts a governed RunAgent, which needs the unattended Do-run lane (plan D10, OD8) first; (3) a judge that prefers the proof bundle and postcondition results (`postcondition.rs:1-40`) with a rubric fallback through inference-core. Budget must come from cost-core run usage and is a no-op on the Subscription route. An attended first slice (L): a goal bar in the Agent Run Console that auto-continues while the session still holds delegated authority.

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-002](#F-t3-002), [F-t3-001](#F-t3-001), [F-t3-018](#F-t3-018), OD8; never auto-continue across approval-required effects.

*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: L · Plane: Model Plane (slides tool, capability-core templates) with Frontend Plane (slide strip, present mode) and Data Plane (reference assets) · Wave: W3 · Confidence: High*

Missing: any manifest, a render step (no renderer in the image, workspace deleted per call), slide strip, present mode and a template skill kind. Keep validation structural (shape bounds, overflow heuristics, notes present) unless [F-t3-014](#F-t3-014) lands a renderer. <a id="F-t3-039"></a>
### F-t3-039 · Runtime release safety: signed candidate and separate rollback artifact, rehearsed rollback, deployed audit fix and a release gate
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: M · Plane: Model Plane (release tooling), Infra Plane for deploy · Wave: W1 · Confidence: Medium*

**What and why.** Run and prove the release tooling that already exists: produce a signed candidate and an independently signed rollback artifact, rehearse rollback against a database snapshot, set numeric canary thresholds, and deploy the audit-publisher fix that sits undeployed. Without a proven rollback Verevon cannot defend its audit and ZDR promises. Quick win.

**Reference practice.** 4 of 12 references ship release safety [SRC]. T3 Code installs the new version beside the old, preflights it, starts a trial child against a database snapshot and rolls back on failure [SRC]. The Claude desktop app ships a signed manifest {version, artifacts[sha256, size], compat} with staged rollout; Hermes restarts drain-first with receipts for interrupted steps [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** `apps/Model Plane/scripts/release-artifact.sh:108-180` already has build, verify, restore and deploy subcommands, signed root-manifest verification and rollback-locator rules, and `scripts/coresystem-conformance.sh:100-125` blocks on missing candidate or rollback evidence. Still open: no signed artifact has been produced (`apps/Model Plane/docs/MODEL_PLANE_RELEASE_EVIDENCE.md:1047` reads absent | absent), the thresholds TSV has no canary values, only migrations 0044 and 0045 have revert scripts, and per postmortem 0002 the publisher fix (commit b9ed9197) and requeue function (migration 0043) are built but not deployed, with 4,992 rows unreplayed. Replace the markdown-row grep in the gate with a check of the signed manifest and rollback evidence files; add error-rate, p95 latency and ZDR-residual thresholds with owner approval; script a rehearsal that restores the rollback artifact and applies reverts to a snapshot, with an expand/contract policy for migrations without reverts; deploy the fix and run the requeue.

**Dependencies and risks.** Signing keys and revert scripts; the gate needs an emergency path, and the evidence register is dated (2026-08-17), so state must be re-verified live.

<a id="F-t3-040"></a>
### F-t3-040 · Eval and replay release gates for prompts, tools and routing: recorded-session replay in CI, required eval stages and an eval gate on RoutingPolicy edits
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: L · Plane: Model Plane (inference-core, eval-lab, orchestrator-core) · Wave: W3 · Confidence: High*

**What and why.** Replay recorded completions through model-gateway in continuous integration (CI), make eval-lab a required stage when prompts or tool schemas change, and put the routing policy behind a candidate, evaluate, promote flow. Verevon edits routing at runtime and changes prompts often; neither path is gated.

**Reference practice.** Replay tests appear in 2 of 12 references and behavioural evals in 3 of 12, a differentiator [SRC]. The DeepSeek harness commits 600 session logs across 227 scenarios, replays them through shipped profiles with an llm-replay provider, tokenises volatile ids, and requires every user-visible change to update a scenario in the same pull request [SRC]. Codex's migration playbook inventories every model usage site and validates against a matrix [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** eval-lab-py ships 14 YAML cases and a report writer, but `.github/workflows/eval-lab.yml:1-60` runs only the credential-free selftest and only on eval-lab changes; no comparison or promotion code exists despite the README claim. RoutingPolicy is one platform-global JSONB row set by `apps/Model Plane/rust/services/session-core/src/routing_policy_grpc.rs:90-165` and hot-swapped by `inference-core/src/http_health.rs:126-175` after serde validation only. Start with synthetic completions served by a replay fake at the InferenceCore gRPC boundary (the MockInference pattern at `execution-core/src/runtime_loop/agent.rs:5546`), outside the production binary; do not make [F-t3-034](#F-t3-034) capture a prerequisite. Widen eval-lab path filters to model-gateway prompt and tool-schema files. Gate the global policy as an async candidate-evaluate-promote flow modelled on the SkillPromotion workflow, not a rejected PUT, because live evals need credentials and spend.

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-042](#F-t3-042), [F-t3-067](#F-t3-067); recorded sessions must be synthetic or scrubbed and never ZDR turns; split experiment ids and user-path budgets into separate S or M items.

*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: L · Plane: Model Plane (inference-core, model-gateway) with Frontend Plane (picker) · Wave: W3 · Confidence: High*

**What and why.** Extend the thin model record with context window, max output and effort ladder, join price from cost-core, add picker search, and give RoutingPolicy a role dimension that replaces hard-coded side-task models, with a purpose tag on every call. The SPA cannot gate controls per model and side tasks pin constants.

**Verevon today and approach.** ListModels returns only id, provider, modality, streaming, features, privacy tier and residency (`apps/Model Plane/proto/model_plane/v1/inference.proto:481-525`), while limits live in ProviderCapabilities (`inference-core/src/provider/mod.rs:395-460`) and prices in cost-core. Two premises were wrong: the browser planner already resolves through the verevon-balance tier (`execution-core/src/llm_planner.rs:105-135`), and gating by features and provider exists. *Verevon today: Partial · Effort: XL · Plane: Model Plane (execution-core, capability-core) with Control Plane (secret store) · Wave: W3 · Confidence: High*

Sandboxes have no network today, which blocks package installs and API work.

The DeepSeek harness reports enforcement as full or partial per call, Claude masks credentials with sentinels and per-host injection, and Antigravity parses shell grammar to suggest "allow always" rules [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** MpNetworkPolicy has Disabled, AllowAll and AllowDomains (`apps/Model Plane/rust/services/execution-core/src/policy.rs:1-60`), but bwrap unshares the network namespace only for Disabled (`sandbox.rs:170-230`), so a proxy env var is advisory. Control grants space:egress for any non-default claim with no org entitlement (`apps/Control Plane/user-core/internal/spaces/space_capability_decision.go:100-175`), contrary to its design doc. No proxy, credential store or enforcement field exists. Split: (a) S: add sandboxed and enforcement fields to CodeResult from ExecOutcome, shown as a badge; (b) L: tier enum plus allowlist digest in the claim, an explicit Control entitlement, netns or firewall enforcement (or the [F-t3-009](#F-t3-009) VM) reusing Quarry's dns_guard; (c) XL, demote: credential-injecting proxy; (d) M: shell-grammar approvals; versioned writes belong to [F-t3-030](#F-t3-030).

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-009](#F-t3-009), [F-t3-032](#F-t3-032); unknown tier means Disabled; any tier above off is refused for ZDR and high-risk runs.

<a id="F-t3-044"></a>
### F-t3-044 · Inline interactive outputs: sandboxed generative-UI widgets with a host-validated prompt bridge, selection-to-agent actions and attributed image results
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: M · Plane: Frontend Plane (widget frame, bridge) with Model Plane (widget artifact kind) and Ingestion Plane (image results) · Wave: W4 · Confidence: High*

**What and why.** Render model-written HTML or SVG widgets inline in the transcript using the existing opaque-origin sandbox, with host theme tokens and a narrow postMessage bridge whose follow-ups appear as visible, rate-limited user turns. Selection-to-context and an attributed image strip are separate pieces. Verevon's sandboxed HTML is confined to the Output panel.

A show_widget tool returns an HTML fragment rendered in a sandboxed iframe under a strict Content Security Policy (CSP), with semantic CSS variables for light and dark, a getState/setState snapshot persisted with the message (up to 16 KiB in Codex), and sendFollowUp gated by a confirmation dialog [SRC][DOC].

**Verevon today and approach.** Model-written HTML already renders through sandboxHtmlDocument (allow-scripts, no allow-same-origin, connect-src none; `apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/src/shared/lib/sandbox-html.ts:1-34`) but only in the Output panel (`ChatArtifactPanel.tsx:495-520`); artifact kinds stop at document, code and html (`apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/sse_events.rs:870-895`). No bridge or selection item exists, and Quarry image results reach only the dashboard search panel. Widget core (M): add a widget kind to the exhaustive ConversationNode registry, mount the iframe only when the artifact is complete and keep it in a keyed cache like ChatChart, because nodes are re-derived on every streamed token. Authenticate bridge events by event.source equal to the iframe contentWindow, since an opaque origin posts "null". Route widget prompts through the normal composer submit path so ZDR, privacy floor and run policy apply. Selection-to-context (S to M) goes with plan I17/OD19; the image strip (S) is a BFF fetch of /api/v1/search/images for web-search turns.

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-031](#F-t3-031), [F-t3-052](#F-t3-052); no widget snapshots or image results for ZDR threads; thumbnails with links only.

*Verevon today: Absent · Effort: L · Plane: Frontend Plane (frame, origin) with Model Plane (MCP host, gate) and Application Plane (storage) and Control Plane (viewer identity) · Wave: W4 · Confidence: Medium*

**What and why.** Render tool-supplied user interfaces (Model Context Protocol (MCP) Apps, ui:// resources) and MCP elicitation forms inside the existing sandboxed frame, with JSON-RPC over postMessage whose tools/call passes the capability gate and approvals. Defer the dedicated user-content origin, viewer-bound connectors and viewer key-value store.

**Reference practice.** MCP Apps appear in 6 of 12 references; elicitation and an artifact host bridge in 2 of 12 each [SRC][DOC]. Tool metadata _meta.ui.resourceUri names a ui:// resource; the host fetches the text/html;profile=mcp-app resource and renders it in an iframe on a separate origin with sandbox=allow-scripts and a CSP from _meta [SRC][DOC]. Codex ships ext-apps and form elicitation [SRC]; ChatGPT web documents the JSON-RPC and CSP model [DOC].

**Verevon today and approach.** A grep for ui://, resourceUri, ext-apps, elicitation/create and sampling/createMessage across Model Plane, frontend and gateway found nothing; the MCP client declares empty capabilities and opens a fresh session per call (`apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/mcp_jsonrpc.rs:30-42`, `mcp_http.rs:1-60`). The "elicitation" at `tool_loop.rs:648-656` is prompt guidance for missing arguments. The only substrate is ArtifactHtmlPreview (`apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/src/features/chat/components/ChatArtifactPanel.tsx:496-530`). The opaque-origin srcdoc frame can host a bridge authenticated by event.source; a separate origin is needed only for same-origin web APIs, SRI-loaded libraries or shareable URLs, an ADR decision [INF, medium confidence]. Merge the bridge with [F-t3-044](#F-t3-044), split elicitation (client capabilities plus the existing approval pause, handled inside the tools/call SSE stream) from MCP Apps, and build an org-restricted per-app CSP. **Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-003](#F-t3-003), [F-t3-044](#F-t3-044), an ADR for the origin; every app-initiated call is attributed to the viewer, never the author; no persistence or key-value store under ZDR.

*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: XL · Plane: Model Plane (snapshots, export, import), Control Plane (grants, audit, admin policy) · Wave: W4 · Confidence: High*

**What and why.** Add an immutable, redacted thread snapshot with org-internal visibility, Control-checked grants and audit, a redacted replay view, an owner-only whole-conversation export, and an importer for ChatGPT and Claude export files. Org-only sharing is expected in B2B and import lowers switching cost.

**Verevon today and approach.** No chat share route exists; user-core grants cover documents only and `apps/Control Plane/user-core/internal/authztaxonomy/resource_taxonomy.json:1-17` lists document as the only ownable type, so a snapshot needs a new classified type kept in parity across the Go embed, Rust mirror and lint script. Replay routes (`apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/http_routes.rs:1243-1340`) are envelope-only by design, so replay must be a new redacted projection. There is no ForkThread RPC (`artifacts.rs:184-204` forks only artifact counters) and no import origin in THREAD_ORIGINS. Per-answer Word, Excel, PowerPoint and print-to-PDF exports ship in the browser (`ChatMessages.tsx:610-630`). Split into three tracks: share snapshot (L, owner-gated by OD19 and plan I19, view-only until I8/I20 lands a fork), export (M, audited through the session audit outbox), import (M, new origin value plus DB CHECK, turns marked untrusted). Refuse all three for ZDR threads; redact or refuse connector content.

<a id="F-t3-047"></a>
### F-t3-047 · Event-triggered automations: normalised connector events from Ingestion wake budgeted runs, with wake gates, dry runs, blueprints and consent-first suggestions
*Verevon today: Absent · Effort: XL · Plane: Model Plane (capability-core triggers) with Ingestion Plane (integration-corev2 events) · Wave: W4 · Confidence: Medium*

**What and why.** Let connector events published by Ingestion wake budgeted Do runs through a trigger record bound to one connection, Space and member, with wake gates, dry runs and per-fire reauthorization; defer blueprints and suggestions. This turns agents from on-demand into proactive, the natural step after scheduled runs.

**Reference practice.** Event triggers appear in 5 of 12 references, wake gates in 3 of 12 [SRC][DOC]. Cursor runs an automations service with event triggers, LLM filtering and dry runs; Hermes adds a source-hash wake gate, a silent sentinel, per-job notes and capped, de-duplicated suggestions [SRC]. ChatGPT MCP Events subscribe with a callback URL and signing secret, verify signed webhooks with a 256 KiB cap, and start a run in the bound conversation [DOC].

**Verevon today and approach.** integration-corev2 publishes webhook_received carrying only eventType, webhookEventId and normalizedBy (`apps/Ingestion Plane/integration-corev2/internal/api/server.go:1050-1100`); the only consumer is Application Plane conversation-core. capability-core has cron_schedules with a per-fire FireAuthorizer (`apps/Model Plane/go/services/capability-core/internal/cron/sweeper.go:14-47`) and a Watch primitive with one process_output adapter and a one-resource, one-Space, one-member model (`internal/watch/watch.go:1-140`), but no trigger records, filters, wake gates or dry runs. Model a trigger as a new Watch source kind or a cron_schedules-like row keyed by (connection_id, Space, member) that implements FireAuthorizer. Consume webhook_received through a durable JetStream consumer (new nats-provisioner binding), fetch the payload by webhookEventId through the integration-corev2 API, dedupe on it as the fire key, classify the payload untrusted before any LLM predicate, and start the run through the scheduled-run path under run budget and org quota caps. Only three event types exist in code and no mail-received event [INF, pattern search], so mail triggers need an Ingestion change first.

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-018](#F-t3-018), [F-t3-002](#F-t3-002); event storms need rate limits; ZDR must come from policy because there is no user turn; trimmed to trigger, consumer, wake gate and budgeted run it is L.

*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: M · Plane: Frontend Plane with Model Plane (optional render hints, narration metadata) · Wave: W3 · Confidence: Medium*

**What and why.** Add a per-user transcript density preference, fold settled turns behind a "Worked for X" row, dock a Step N of M plan strip in the chat transcript, and narrate tool rows with per-action sentences. Long agent transcripts are where readability decides whether users trust the agent.

### F-t3-049 · Typed run lifecycle events and status cues: retry, compaction, context-change rows, thread status, connector incidents
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: L · Plane: Model Plane (model-gateway, execution-core, capability-core) with Frontend Plane · Wave: W2 · Confidence: Medium*

**What and why.** Add the missing lifecycle events to ChatEvent: retry_scheduled, a compaction marker with counts only, and context-change or notice rows (history shed on overflow, skill guidance truncated, trigger kind). Derive one thread status with fixed precedence and an unseen-completion state. Today retries, history shedding and compaction are silent, so users see a stall with no explanation.

**Reference practice.** Retries with typed end reasons, compaction markers and status pills each appear in 1 of 12 references; message source and connector incidents in 2 of 12. DeepSeek appends a retry event before waiting, emits a CompactionItem with token counts and types every message source [SRC]. <a id="F-t3-050"></a>
### F-t3-050 · Scrubbable browser-run replay with per-action briefs and a walkthrough clip in the run proof
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: M · Plane: Frontend Plane (scrubber) with Ingestion Plane (Quarry recording) and Model Plane (proof bundle) · Wave: W4 · Confidence: High*

**What and why.** Add playback and a time slider to the existing browser step timeline, show a one-line brief per action in the Run Console and chat rail, and attach a walkthrough clip reference to the proof bundle. It is the best answer to "what did the agent do on that site", and the event data already exists.

**Reference practice.** Scrubbable replay is unique to Manus (1 of 12 references); recordings as a habit appear in 2 of 12. T3 Code builds a timeline slider from toolUsed events and records browser frames at 12 fps [SRC]; Cursor makes recordings routine [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** Model Plane emits dispatched (with reason), decided and observation events linked by action_id with screenshot_ref (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/execution-core/src/browser_events.rs:100-215), and BrowserChrome has a step timeline with rationale and screenshot (apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/src/features/dashboard/home/BrowserChrome.tsx:457-515). Missing: a slider or auto-play, the brief in the Run Console, and any clip: Quarry captures one screencast frame at a time (apps/Ingestion Plane/Quarry-v2/crates/quarry-browser/src/chromiumoxide.rs:1700-1760) and RunProofBundle has no browser section (apps/Model Plane/proto/model_plane/v1/proof_bundle.proto:30-52). Extend BrowserChrome, not a second timeline. Assemble a filmstrip from the per-step screenshots Quarry already stores before building continuous video [INF]; the scrubber alone is S and a quick win. The bundle carries a reference, never bytes.

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-008](#F-t3-008), [F-t3-034](#F-t3-034); no clip exists under ZDR by construction; a retention and redaction policy must precede shipping.

<a id="F-t3-051"></a>
### F-t3-051 · Images as a governed agent tool: typed generation, explicit provider choice, an edit endpoint with a mark tool, and C2PA provenance
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: XL · Plane: Model Plane (model-gateway, inference-core) with Frontend Plane (mark tool) and Control Plane (signing certificate) · Wave: W4 · Confidence: Medium*

**What and why.** Expose generate_image to the chat tool loop and the Do loop with a typed result, a per-run semaphore, cost accounting and no silent fallback; later add an edit endpoint with a mark tool, C2PA (Coalition for Content Provenance and Authenticity) signing and a second provider. Without this, image mode is a dead end and reports cannot include generated visuals.

**Reference practice.** Generation is table-stakes (4 of 11 references), editing common (4 of 11), C2PA unique (1 of 11). Codex returns a typed result and asks before falling back to a weaker model [SRC]; Cursor caps concurrent image calls per run [SRC]. Manus draws marks as masks for edit instructions [SRC, DOC], and Claude desktop ships a Rust/WASM C2PA module [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** Generation is a request flag that takes over the turn via image_gen_stream with hard-coded 1024x1024 (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/sse.rs:1415-1440,5945-6130); inference-core has one provider family that silently walks the chain (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/inference-core/src/provider/vision.rs:1-60,130-160); cost-core has no image pricing and Model Plane has no durable binary artifact store. The revised prompt is already surfaced as the artifact title. Sequence: (1) agent tool with semaphore, priced through cost-core and gated by ZDR and capability policy (M); (2) explicit provider selection surfacing model_used; (3) decide the durable home for binary artifacts; (4) C2PA signing where bytes are produced; (5) mark tool and edit; (6) Gemini adapter.

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-017](#F-t3-017), [F-t3-041](#F-t3-041); generate_image is refused on ZDR turns today; split the agent tool from edit and provenance.

<a id="F-t3-052"></a>
### F-t3-052 · Composer: atomic typed context tokens, per-thread drafts and thread references
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: L · Plane: Frontend Plane (editor, drafts) with Model Plane (reference resolution, thread tools) · Wave: W3 · Confidence: Medium*

**What and why.** Make references to Data Plane documents, past threads, Spaces and skills explicit typed tokens in the send payload, persist a text draft per chat thread, and let the agent browse referenced threads through read-only outline, search and read tools. Typed references suit Verevon's authority model, and losing a long prompt on reload is a top frustration.

**Reference practice.** A typed composer is table-stakes (6 of 12 references); drafts and thread references appear in 2 of 12 each. T3 Code uses decorator nodes per token type and versioned localStorage drafts with byte budgets [SRC]. Manus lets a task reference up to 20 task ids checked lazily at read time, with outline, search and read tools instead of injected content [SRC, DOC].

**Verevon today and approach.** Slash commands with a server catalogue and an entity overlay ship, but the composer is a textarea plus overlay and @ is reserved for Space agents (apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/src/features/dashboard/home/DashboardComposer.tsx:3874-3885); only Home persists a draft (apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/src/shared/session/home-draft-storage.ts:1-78) while the chat page keeps in-memory state (ChatPage.tsx:925-945); no thread references exist. Send a structured context_refs array beside plain text, rendered by the existing overlay, before committing to a rich-text editor; generalise home-draft-storage to a per-thread key with its retention gate and sign-out purge (S, quick win); build thread references on [F-t3-012](#F-t3-012). Large-file handoff belongs to [F-t3-017](#F-t3-017) and targets imports-core.

**Dependencies and risks.** Use a trigger other than @; refuse ZDR threads for references; referenced thread content is untrusted data; Space access never widens an owner-plane ACL.

### F-t3-053 · Claim-level citations: bind chips by server number, a cited-passage viewer and a provenance stamp on research artifacts
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: M · Plane: Model Plane (anchors) with Frontend Plane (chips, viewer) and Data Plane v2 (passage reads) · Wave: W3 · Confidence: Medium*

**What and why.** Bind citation chips by the number the server already emits, open the cited passage from a chip, and stamp a provenance record on research artifacts. Verevon's sources panel is already ahead of most references; the missing link is from a claim to the exact passage.

**Reference practice.** Passage-level source viewing appears in 2 of 11 references, file chips that open at a line in 2 of 12. ChatGPT opens connected files beside the chat and Perplexity offers a per-claim Check Sources view; both rest on public docs only [DOC]. T3 Code's file chips open at a line [SRC]; Claude Science describes versioned artifacts with provenance records [DOC].

**Verevon today and approach.** The server numbers citations and emits claim_id and offsets (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/sse_events.rs:533-546), but the client drops number (apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/src/shared/api/chat-client.ts:953-969) and binds [n] by array position (chat-media-markdown.tsx:1250-1268). KB grounding already carries document_id, chunk_index and fact text (retrieval.rs:84-146) while the Sources tab shows only a count (ChatPanels.tsx:476). Export is shipped client-side and leaves this item. Sequence: add number to the client model (planned I6, S); build the passage viewer for KB sources from facts already on the wire; add a web locator from the Quarry artifact id for non-ZDR turns. Defer web authority labels (no domain-class data exists) and the paid per-claim support check.

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-027](#F-t3-027); viewers open only documents the viewer may read through the Data Plane read API; no stored web page exists on ZDR turns.

<a id="F-t3-054"></a>
### F-t3-054 · Web reading upgrade: chunked long-page reader and a batched, reference-id web tool
*Verevon today: Absent · Effort: M · Plane: Model Plane · Wave: W3 · Confidence: High*

**What and why.** Stop cutting fetched pages at 4,000 characters: return an outline, the first chunk and a reference id, and add read_page and find_in_page over the full text with no new egress. Let web_search take up to four queries and return one merged, de-duplicated list with stable reference ids. B2B users research long primary sources, and reference ids make the cited page the page the model opened.

**Reference practice.** Batched operations and chunked fetch each appear in 2 of 11 references. DeepSeek stores long pages, returns an outline plus chunk 1 with view_chunk(n), and runs 1 to 4 de-duplicated queries, interleaved by rank and capped at 8 [SRC]. Codex uses typed operations with reference ids so open, click and find act on an id, not a URL [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** fetch_url truncates at MAX_FETCH_CHARS = 4,000 (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/tool_loop.rs:124-126,2987-3055), web_search takes one query (tool_loop.rs:2874-2950), no read_page, find_in_page or queries[] exists, and deep research shares the same cap (deep_research.rs:165-176). Back read_page and find_in_page with the Quarry artifact id the gateway already resolves (quarry.rs:606-650,780-840), with a turn-scoped in-memory buffer only under ZDR [INF]; rank with grounding::select_passages. Parallel web_search calls already run concurrently via join_all (tool_loop.rs:7204-7210), so queries[] saves tokens and rounds, not latency. Decide whether the execution-core Do loop (8,000-character cap, web_tools.rs:38) is in scope.

**Dependencies and risks.** SSRF vetting stays in Quarry; every chunk stays untrusted and screened; wider fan-out must respect allow_paid_providers and org quota.

<a id="F-t3-055"></a>
### F-t3-055 · Web access governance and provider transparency: org web policy, domain rules, cached-only mode and "Searched via" disclosure
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: L · Plane: Control Plane (policy authority) with Model Plane and Quarry (enforcement) · Wave: W3 · Confidence: High*

**What and why.** Add an org web policy (web on or off, allowed and blocked domains, paid providers, cached-only mode, provider pin) stored in org-core, enforced in both web tool loops and re-checked by Quarry, and make routing visible with a provider-and-reason field and a "Searched via X" line. Enterprise admins need to restrict where agents read and see where queries go.

**Reference practice.** Governance appears in 3 of 11 references, the provider seam and data-handling disclosure in 1 of 11 each. Claude desktop has an org capability switch, per-domain fetch consent with an admin-skippable preflight and egress allow-lists [SRC]. Codex offers disabled, cached or live search modes [SRC]; DeepSeek names the provider with explicit selection states [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** A per-turn web toggle and RetrievalScope are enforced in the inline loop (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/tool_loop.rs:1420-1480), paid providers are gated by tier and ZDR (quarry.rs:180-275), and Quarry's host list is a security feed, not org-configurable (apps/Ingestion Plane/Quarry-v2/crates/quarry-security/src/preflight.rs:1-60). No org policy, consent, cached-only mode, router decision or disclosure exists, and execution-core web_tools.rs:240-262 sends only query, limit and zdr. Read the policy through a narrow fail-closed service endpoint like the retention posture, not a bearer claim; pass a policy revision to Quarry on every call. Ship "Searched via" first (S to M). Verify the provider table against smart_router.rs before publishing it.

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-032](#F-t3-032); cached-only mode must add no egress; per-domain prompts only on Do runs.

<a id="F-t3-056"></a>
### F-t3-056 · Connectors: in-chat suggestion cards with resumable connect, and editable write confirmation with user-visible policy levels
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: L · Plane: Model Plane · Wave: W3 · Confidence: High*

**What and why.** Let the model return a provider key and reason that the frontend renders as an opt-in card, with a connect intent that resumes the turn after OAuth; and show an editable preview card for mail drafts and calendar events before a write, with user-visible per-tool policy levels. Editable cards are the difference between a trusted and a feared "send on my behalf".

**Reference practice.** Suggestions appear in 4 of 11 references, write policy and cards in 2 of 11. Claude desktop gives the model suggest_connectors, keeps the connect intent across the login redirect and uses a four-level tool policy [SRC]. Manus emits editable email-draft and calendar-event cards and applies exactly the confirmed payload [SRC]. Claude.ai never ranks by payment [DOC].

**Verevon today and approach.** Read/write classification already exists twice (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/execution-core/src/integration_tools.rs:60-71,747-769; integration-corev2 Capability.Direction) and Do-mode writes pause for durable approval with a frozen, fingerprint-bound descriptor (runtime_loop/agent.rs:4594-4650). Missing: an editable preview (the card offers only approve or reject, ChatMessages.tsx:1890-1925), any suggestion tool or connect-intent record, and user-visible policy levels. Reuse the connect-session routes the gateway already proxies; do not add an integration-corev2 client to model-gateway. Unify the execution-core catalogue with Direction, build cards on planned G8 and G13, and map user levels onto the existing Ask decision and autonomy rung.

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-010](#F-t3-010), [F-t3-003](#F-t3-003); an edited payload must produce a new descriptor and fingerprint; approval continuations are refused under ZDR; writes default to always-ask.

<a id="F-t3-057"></a>
### F-t3-057 · Custom connectors: probe-and-test add flow, secret-reference MCP auth, and a REST/API-key connector kind behind an egress proxy
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: L · Plane: Model Plane with Ingestion Plane (integration-corev2 executor and egress proxy) · Wave: W4 · Confidence: High*

**What and why.** Add a probe that returns an MCP (Model Context Protocol) server's tools and auth requirement without saving, a tool picker, a secret-reference step for static-token servers, and a REST/API-key connector kind executed by a server-side proxy that injects vaulted secrets. This lets a business plug in its own ERP, CRM and internal APIs while keeping keys out of model context.

**Reference practice.** Custom connectors appear in 4 of 12 references, host-brokered secrets in 3 of 12. Manus validates the server, auto-detects OAuth, inspects tools before save and supports API connectors with secrets [SRC]. Claude desktop injects vaulted credentials with per-operation confirm rules and a network allow-list [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** Register, share, OAuth and a one-call connect exist, and connect auto-discovers tools/list (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/http_routes.rs:2771-2870), but there is no probe or tool picker (McpServersSection.tsx:150-215), and validate_mcp_server rejects any raw token, so static bearer servers cannot be registered at all (runtime_registries.rs:410-470). Implement the probe over http_list_tools and discover_protected_resource. Put the REST executor and proxy in integration-corev2, reusing its SSRF client, allowlist and vault. The sandbox proxy-only namespace is a separate item: AllowDomains is advisory and code_interpreter forces Disabled (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/execution-core/src/code_interpreter.rs:575-583).

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-003](#F-t3-003), [F-t3-043](#F-t3-043); registration is refused under ZDR; loose allow-lists make the proxy an exfiltration path; secrets never reach logs.

<a id="F-t3-058"></a>
### F-t3-058 · One versioned lifecycle for authored behaviours (skills, slash templates, presets) with a /learn command and curation
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: XL · Plane: Model Plane (capability-core) with Frontend Plane (libraries) · Wave: W3 · Confidence: Medium*

**What and why.** Give skills immutable versions with digests pinned on runs, draft and publish, Space and org libraries, import, authored presets, a linter on every upsert path, and a /learn command and Save-as-skill action through the proposal path. Without versions a skill edit silently changes every run that matches it.

**Reference practice.** Skill distribution appears in 6 of 12 references, presets and agent-assisted authoring in 4 of 12, /learn in 3 of 12, authoring doctrine only in Hermes. Manus keeps personal, team, project and official libraries with versions, restore and project lock [SRC]. Hermes /learn takes a directory, URL, notes or "what we just did" and writes a lean index with per-chapter references [SRC]. Claude desktop refuses an org skill whose SHA-256 differs [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** agent_skills is unique on (org, name) and UpsertAgentSkill overwrites in place (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/session-core/src/grpc.rs:5075-5115); the learning loop persists candidates enabled with no proposal step (apps/Model Plane/go/services/capability-core/internal/skillsink/sink.go:41-62); a versioned skill_packages schema exists unused (capability-core migrations/0003_capabilities_registry.up.sql:134-182); presets are hard-coded (preset-agents.ts:1-40). Choose one owner and table first. Ship the thin layer (doctrine, linter, /learn, Save-as-skill, S to M) because it needs no schema change, then versions with ContentHash as digest and the quarantine re-keyed by version, then libraries and import. Make the background loop propose rather than enable.

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-014](#F-t3-014), [F-t3-007](#F-t3-007); /learn must reuse the sessionreview rule (refuse unless zdr:false); Space libraries need a schema change; org promotion stays an admin decision.

<a id="F-t3-059"></a>
### F-t3-059 · Plugin manifest loader with supply-chain pins, cross-vendor adapters and an org-curated catalogue
*Verevon today: Stub · Effort: L · Plane: Model Plane (capability-core) with Control Plane (entitlement and audit) · Wave: W4 · Confidence: High*

**What and why.** Build the loader that expands a plugin into child capabilities (skills, remote HTTP MCP servers, slash commands first), pins every source to a commit SHA or archive digest with a kill list, lists every component at install, and starts with an org-curated catalogue. A plugin is how a vertical solution reaches an org.

**Reference practice.** Plugin packages are table-stakes (7 of 12 references), marketplaces and cross-vendor compatibility common (4 of 12), supply-chain safety a differentiator (3 of 12). Codex accepts the open agent-plugins.org 1.0 manifest with foreign-namespace adapters and atomic upgrade with rollback [SRC]. Hermes pins about 570 catalogue entries to a 40-hex commit, clones every entry in CI and keeps a removed.yaml kill list [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** capability-core stores plugin_packages with manifest_json, risk_level and rollout_state but no digest, source or signature column (apps/Model Plane/go/services/capability-core/migrations/0003_capabilities_registry.up.sql:240-259), and the handler says registration is inert until a host loads the manifest, which nothing does (internal/api/plugins.go:13-22). Keep catalogue and loader in capability-core and use Control Plane for entitlement and audit only. Expand skills, remote MCP (through normalizeMCPRegistration) and commands into existing registries and refuse stdio, bin and script components with a report. Add digest, source and kill-list columns; defer foreign-layout adapters until the internal manifest is stable. Sequence after [F-t3-058](#F-t3-058).

<a id="F-t3-060"></a>
### F-t3-060 · Scoped memory for Spaces, Projects and named agents with isolation
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: L · Plane: Model Plane (session-core); Frontend Plane for the agent Memory tab · Wave: W3 · Confidence: Medium*

**What and why.** Give agent_memory an explicit scope kind and scope id (user, space, project, agent), filter recall by the active scope plus allowed parents, and add an isolation flag so nothing flows in from broader scopes or out to them. Business users mix clients and projects; scoped memory prevents cross-project leakage while keeping the rule that Space access never widens an owner-plane ACL (access control list).

**Reference practice.** Scoped memory ships in 5 of 12 references; named persistent agents in 4 of 12. ChatGPT's project-only memory defines the isolation contract: no outside memory comes in, nothing goes out, and the project summary is regenerated from that project's chats only [DOC]. Cursor gives an agent principal a small home store whose SELF.md is injected into each run and which the agent can edit [SRC]. Antigravity adds typed scopes with activation triggers and globs [DOC].

**Verevon today and approach.** agent_memory has a free-text scope column with no CHECK (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/session-core/migrations/0004_tasks_cron_hooks_skills_memory.sql:210-232), and Dreaming loads pending messages across all threads with no Space predicate (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/session-core/src/dreaming.rs:1263-1285), so Space-thread content can feed personal user-scope memory and be recalled elsewhere (impact is inference; worth a test). Projects are client-only localStorage. Order the work: (1) S fail-closed fix excluding Space-origin and agent_run threads from user-scope extraction and recall, with a regression test; (2) M Space scope read and written only on turns carrying a Control-signed Space decision, reusing the threads.source_scope='conversation' isolation pattern; (3) project scope after server Projects (I21), ideally mapped onto Spaces; (4) agent scope last. The semantic tier supports equality filters only, so parent-scope composition must happen in session-core Postgres.

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-013](#F-t3-013), [F-t3-001](#F-t3-001), [F-t3-020](#F-t3-020); Space-scoped rows need erasure and Space-deletion coverage; agent memory must never carry authority; ZDR (Zero-Data-Retention) handling of agent memory needs a decision.

<a id="F-t3-061"></a>
### F-t3-061 · Personalisation that tailors itself: a standing user profile in the cached prefix, tone presets, a personal onboarding step and trustworthy settings
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: L · Plane: Model Plane (session-core memory, resolver) with Control Plane (settings) and Frontend Plane (onboarding, settings) · Wave: W3 · Confidence: Medium*

**What and why.** Synthesise one bounded, user-editable profile per user from accepted memories and inject it as a stable block in the cached prompt prefix, with per-turn recall after the cache breakpoint. Add professional tone presets, timezone and locale in the prompt, a personal first-run step (name, role, language) and settings search. An assistant that learns and tailors itself is part of the stated vision.

**Reference practice.** A standing user model is a differentiator (2 of 12); personality presets are common (5 of 12); onboarding is table-stakes (10 of 12). Hermes loads curated memory (about 2,200 characters) and a user profile (about 1,375 characters) into a cached block at session start, writes only to storage and the tool result so the prefix cache survives, and rebuilds at compaction [SRC]. ChatGPT offers seven base styles plus sliders applied to all chats [DOC]. DeepSeek keeps an ordered onboarding ledger with per-step complete or skip, stored per user and versioned [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** Dreaming already extracts inferred user-scope memories (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/session-core/src/dream_extractor.rs:1-140), but the per-turn memory block changes every turn (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/sse.rs:1655-1680) and Anthropic caches one joined system string (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/inference-core/src/provider/anthropic.rs:284-306), so cross-turn cache hits are defeated. Persona (1,500 characters), verbosity and typed settings actions exist; no profile, presets, timezone or settings search. Build the profile writer as a slower second pass in the Dreaming loop, not in MemoryConsolidationWorkflow (org-scoped, DeniesZDR, and scheduled fires are rejected). Split system content into a stable block and a dynamic block after the breakpoint. Slices: profile plus stable segment (M), presets plus timezone and locale via the resolver (S), first-run step (S), settings search and effective-privacy view (S, overlaps [F-t3-068](#F-t3-068)).

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-001](#F-t3-001), [F-t3-020](#F-t3-020), [F-t3-022](#F-t3-022); never synthesise a profile from Space-origin threads; caching is off under ZDR; profiles stay user-editable and provenance-labelled.

<a id="F-t3-062"></a>
### F-t3-062 · Enterprise compliance posture: retention windows and legal hold, data-use disclosure, regime tags, sharing policy, telemetry scrub, signed releases
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: XL · Plane: Control Plane (retention policy, regime tags); Model Plane (sweeper, provider metadata, share links); Support Plane (telemetry, release pipeline) · Wave: W3 · Confidence: Medium*

**What and why.** Add per-org retention windows and an org or thread legal hold that a session-core sweeper obeys (dry-run, audited, propagating deletes to Data Plane indexes and derived memory), show each provider's data-use posture in the model picker, generalise the ZDR gate into org regime tags, design share links with an org-only default, scrub telemetry through a typed event registry and build a signed release in CI. Enterprises require retention and hold before broad deployment, and signed releases underpin credible security claims.

**Reference practice.** Retention is common (4 of 12), telemetry privacy 5 of 12, release integrity 5 of 12; data-use controls 3 of 12; regime tags and sharing governance 1 of 12 each. Claude resolves retention policy per surface with legal hold and a headless sweep, latches a restricted verdict from server-side org taints, and scrubs typed telemetry per sink [SRC]. Hermes pins dependencies by SHA and enforces a minimum release age [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** Shipped: GDPR erasure fan-out (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/session-core/src/gdpr.rs:1-30), a Control-only Space-scoped legal hold (apps/Control Plane/user-core/internal/spaces/deletion_authorization.go:19-45), an env-driven audit retention sweeper and release-artifact v3 tooling that no CI workflow invokes (apps/Model Plane/scripts/release-artifact.sh:1-60). Missing: per-org retention for threads and runs (org-core DataRetentionDays is unused), org or thread hold, data-use statements, regime tags, share links, a telemetry registry, and SHA-pinned actions (none of 107 uses lines). Split into independent workstreams: retention plus hold with dry-run and audit (L), data-use label (S), regime tags (M), share-link ADR then build with the grant issued in Control (L), telemetry registry (M), release pipeline (M). Reuse the Space legal-hold contract rather than a new flag shape.

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-005](#F-t3-005), [F-t3-004](#F-t3-004); hold versus GDPR erasure precedence needs a Control decision; ZDR runs hold no content, so return a typed refusal; do not claim signed releases before a candidate with rollback exists.

<a id="F-t3-063"></a>
### F-t3-063 · Loop guard, durable org hooks and universal tool-output handles in both loops
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: L · Plane: Model Plane · Wave: W2 · Confidence: Medium*

**What and why.** Three independent wiring jobs on existing primitives: a shared LoopGuard that detects repeated failing calls and stops with a typed loop_detected reason; durable org and Space hook rules that reach Do runs; and tool-output handles for every large built-in result. A run that repeats the same failing call wastes tokens and is what users describe as "it keeps doing the same thing", and hooks let admins enforce policy without code changes.

**Reference practice.** Loop hygiene, hooks and output spill are each differentiators (3 of 12). Hermes runs a side-effect-free loop controller for repeat, cycle and no-progress detection with stop gates [SRC]. DeepSeek injects escalating reminders at 3, 5 and 8 repeats and spills oversized output to private files behind opaque locators within a shared token budget [SRC]. Cursor registers generic and tool-specific pre and post hooks returning allow, deny or ask [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** Neither loop detects repetition beyond exact duplicate read-only calls (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/tool_loop.rs:2320-2345; apps/Model Plane/rust/services/execution-core/src/runtime_loop/agent.rs:3456-3473). The hook engine is real, but rules live in an in-memory gateway registry and the agent loop passes an empty hook_context (agent.rs:1923-1927) because RunAgentRequest has no hook field; a session-core hook_configs table exists but nothing reads it. Handles already park oversized built-in reads in the inline loop, but not fetch_url, knowledge_search or provider results, and execution-core only truncates at 8,000 characters. Ship the LoopGuard first, built on duplicate_call_signature and retrieval_signature in mp-contracts with the retry.rs risky-call classifier so reminders never replay side effects. Then pick one durable rule owner, add a REST and settings route and a hook_context field on RunAgentRequest. Finally extend handles to the remaining tools and add an execution-core store.

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-032](#F-t3-032); hooks may only narrow authority and rule order must be validated; no handles under ZDR; hook webhooks are egress and need allow-listing.

<a id="F-t3-064"></a>
### F-t3-064 · Public, versioned Agent API with origin-tagged runs as the one entry for every surface (SPA, API keys, schedules, mail, later channels)
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: L · Plane: Model Plane (model-gateway); Control Plane for keys; Channel Plane (not yet built) for adapters · Wave: W4 · Confidence: Medium*

**What and why.** Expose a versioned public agent API (create run, stream, resume, cancel, answer an interaction) behind Control-issued API keys exchanged for audience-bound bearers, and record an origin and parent id on every run. Enterprise customers and internal surfaces (add-ins, mail tasks, cron) then reuse the governed Do-mode pipeline instead of re-implementing it.

**Reference practice.** One runtime behind many thin surfaces is table-stakes (6 of 12). Codex exposes exec, review, resume and fork by id plus an app-server daemon from one Rust binary, so each surface is a thin client [SRC]. Manus treats chats, API tasks, schedules and sub-tasks as tasks with a task_url [SRC]. Hermes channel adapters normalise, authorise and resolve a canonical session key per platform chat [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** model-gateway already serves the run lifecycle (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/http_routes.rs:132-236) and an OpenAPI spec with generated TypeScript and Python SDKs exists (apps/Model Plane/api/generate-sdk.sh:1-28), but only the cookie-authenticated BFF (backend for frontend) consumes it. auth-core's API-key plugin has no consumer that exchanges a key for a bearer. The mail-task pipeline is wired to None (apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/apps/gateway/src/config.rs:269) and a scheduled step is one no-tool 1,024-token call (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/execution-core/src/grpc.rs:498-531). Do not host the API on model-gateway: org, Space and persona layers and the org ZDR raise are applied by the BFF, so host the surface at the Frontend Plane gateway or a thin edge that reuses its normalisation. Record origin in run metadata rather than widening the six-value thread-origin CHECK. Slices: API-key edge plus facade; governed unattended scheduled loop; mail-task wiring; channel adapters and CLI deferred until a Channel Plane exists.

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-002](#F-t3-002), [F-t3-016](#F-t3-016), [F-t3-067](#F-t3-067); unattended runs need always-ask or pre-approved plans; API-started browser runs still need broker grants.

<a id="F-t3-065"></a>
### F-t3-065 · Declarative provider registry and bring-your-own connections
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: XL · Plane: Model Plane (registry) with Integration Core (credentials) · Wave: W4 · Confidence: High*

**What and why.** Turn provider registration from environment configuration into data with an admin overlay, then add bring-your-own (BYO) connections held by Integration Core (API key, cloud role or customer gateway) referenced by opaque connection id. Enterprise and sovereign customers can add their own gateway or cloud tenancy without a Verevon release, and heavy users can bring their own keys.

**Reference practice.** A provider registry is common (5 of 12); BYO credentials are table-stakes (6 of 12); multiple instances of one provider 1 of 12. Hermes keeps 41 overridable provider profiles, each with api mode, auth type, base and models URLs and error classification, overridable by a user profile of the same name [SRC]. Claude desktop takes BYO cloud credentials from a key, cloud role or helper script with TTL and silent refresh, probes health on save, and never lets platform and customer credentials fall back to each other [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** Providers come only from from_env (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/inference-core/src/config.rs:200-335), with ZDR as an evidence-bound attestation (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/inference-core/src/provider/zdr.rs:1-50). The only per-user connection is the ChatGPT subscription, where inference-core receives an opaque subscription_connection_id (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/inference-core/src/provider/codex_subscription.rs:1-70). No profile table, discovery, admin overlay, BYOK or customer gateway. Phase it: (1) an admin overlay over env-defined providers (hide, reorder, custom model ids) stored like the session-core routing-policy singleton (M); (2) one OpenAI-compatible customer-gateway profile with a secret reference and lowest privacy tier; (3) cloud-role BYO last. Do not replace env wiring with a generic registry: attestation and provider-specific adapters cannot be expressed as compat switches.

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-042](#F-t3-042) and an ADR on model entitlement ownership; an admin form must never be able to assert ZDR or residency; customer-billed routes need cost-core attribution and the served-by line must name the connection.

<a id="F-t3-066"></a>
### F-t3-066 · UI quality gates: remove Norwegian-only controls and enforce i18n and design tokens in CI
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: S · Plane: Frontend Plane · Wave: W1 · Confidence: High*

**What and why.** Route the remaining Norwegian-only strings (approval buttons, plan rung labels, status words, trace copy) through i18n.tr, then add a lint gate so they cannot return, plus a scoped raw-colour rule and a per-surface state checklist. Language leaks in approval controls are visible to every English user and to screen readers, and a fast-growing SolidJS UI needs automated guards.

**Reference practice.** Internationalisation (i18n) is table-stakes (7 of 12); design-token enforcement is common (4 of 12). DeepSeek keeps typed dictionaries per feature namespace, fails CI on string literals in JSX outside the i18n call, and asserts key parity across locales [SRC]. Hermes layers locale packs and keeps a DESIGN.md with a state checklist [SRC]. Claude desktop ships about 34k ICU strings in 11 locales [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** Confirmed bare literals: the Godkjenn and Avvis approval buttons (apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/src/features/chat/components/ChatMessages.tsx:1917,1924), rung labels (:2536-2542), Last ned (apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/src/features/chat/components/ChatArtifactPanel.tsx:397,581) and English-only Pause fleet (apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/src/features/agents/components/FleetRunConsole.tsx:150-158), while about 984 other strings use i18n.tr. eslint.config.js has no JSX-literal or raw-colour rule and no stylelint exists. Fix the roughly 15 to 20 leaking sites first. Then add JSXText and JSXAttribute literal selectors in a second config block scoped to features/chat and features/agents that re-spreads the existing selectors, because a second no-restricted-syntax block on the same files replaces the first. Drop the parity test and namespace catalogs: inline tr(no, en) is the documented convention. Scope the raw-colour rule to features/chat or use a baseline, since features/agents holds about 537 hex literals. The state checklist needs its own document; DESIGN.md is the marketing-site spec.

**Dependencies and risks.** None material; keep new rules scoped to new or touched files to avoid gate noise.

<a id="F-t3-067"></a>
### F-t3-067 · Contract and architecture guardrails in CI: generated SPA types from Rust, plane-boundary and logging gates, and a size ratchet
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: L · Plane: Frontend Plane (types); repo-root CI gates (no plane owner) · Wave: W1 · Confidence: High*

**What and why.** Check SSE (server-sent events) payload fields between the Rust emitter and the SPA parser in CI, add hermetic syntactic gates for no cross-plane DB access and no raw user content in logs, and add a baselined file and function size ratchet. This turns plane-ownership and ZDR rules, enforced today by review and by docs that go stale, into durable checks that hold when AI agents write most of the code.

**Reference practice.** Schema codegen is common (4 of 12); AST fitness gates are a differentiator (3 of 12). Hermes keeps one Pydantic model per method and event, renders TypeScript types and an OpenRPC document, rejects extra keys, and reads the ratchet level from main so a PR cannot relax its own check [SRC]. DeepSeek runs AST-based repository checks that renamed imports cannot dodge, with SHA-256 baselines for legacy violations [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** Event-name coverage is already a CI gate (.github/workflows/sse-taxonomy.yml:44-62 runs gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py --check with a step proving it can fail), contract goldens are sha256-pinned and buf breaking runs. Missing: field-level payload types (ChatEvent::payload() hand-builds json! at apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/sse_events.rs:490-500; no ts-rs, schemars, utoipa or specta anywhere under apps), any cross-plane DB or log-content gate, any size ratchet (agent.rs 13,521 lines, sse.rs 13,325, http_routes.rs 11,681) and Model Plane clippy. Split into three: (1) extend gen-sse-event-taxonomy.py to extract per-event field lists from the payload() arms and compare with chat-client.ts reads, or convert payloads to serde structs first, with an unknown-key validator in tests only (S to M); (2) hermetic gates modelled on scripts/tests with self-tests, cross-plane DB scan first; (3) size ratchet last, baselined by hash like apps/Control Plane/auth-core/scripts/lint-ratchet.mjs.

**Dependencies and risks.** Support Plane is the RustDesk relay, not tooling, so gates live in repo-root scripts; buf.yml passes an unquoted path containing a space, so verify it checks what it appears to (inference).

<a id="F-t3-068"></a>
### F-t3-068 · Policy and data-flow transparency: effective-policy origin view and declared egress catalogue
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: L · Plane: Frontend Plane BFF (aggregation) with each owner plane, Control Plane (org toggles) and repo-level tooling · Wave: W3 · Confidence: Medium*

**What and why.** Show the effective value of approval posture, ZDR, privacy tier, routing and budgets for a Space or thread together with the layer that set it, and declare every outbound destination per plane in an egress catalogue that drives a generated "what leaves the platform" table and a CI check. Verevon's privacy positioning rests on ZDR, residency and one-way-tightening policy, but admins cannot see why a setting has its value or which providers receive which data.

**Reference practice.** Egress disclosure is common (4 of 12); configuration provenance is a differentiator (3 of 12). Claude keeps one declarative catalogue listing each destination with its disabling toggle, contacting process and CSP (Content Security Policy) directive, and generates CSP, allow-lists, admin toggles and local 503 fallbacks from it [SRC]. DeepSeek's --dump-config applies layers cumulatively and attributes each field's origin and patchedBy [SRC].

**Verevon today and approach.** Per-answer served-by provenance (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/sse_events.rs:262-296), one-way ratchets for posture (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/profile.rs:1-110) and ZDR (apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/apps/gateway/src/domains/chat/shared.rs:35-118), a router-policy read and a Trust Center exist. Missing: any value-with-origin view, any egress catalogue (the only CSP is nginx.conf connect-src 'self' https:, which governs the browser, not plane-to-provider traffic) and a generated disclosure. No single Model Plane resolver can see every layer without cross-plane DB access, so each owner plane exposes its own {value, origin} and a read-only BFF route aggregates; Model Plane returns only posture floor, privacy-tier floor, routing and budgets. Put the catalogue and CI check in repo-level scripts beside coresystem-conformance.sh. Slices: effective-policy panel (M); catalogue plus CI (M); browser CSP tightening separately.

**Dependencies and risks.** [F-t3-006](#F-t3-006), [F-t3-005](#F-t3-005); members must not see admin-only or other-tenant policy; the disclosure is a public claim, so generate it and never hand-edit.

<a id="F-t3-016"></a>
### F-t3-016 · Multi-replica-safe run control: shared cancel, queued-input, plan-mode and turn state, durable send receipts and an editable message queue
*Verevon today: Partial · Effort: M · Plane: Model Plane (model-gateway, session-core) with Frontend Plane (queue dock) · Wave: W1 · Confidence: Medium · moved down from Priority by verification*

**What and why.** Move process-local run control in model-gateway (cancel flags, queued input, plan-mode and team-worker state, the chat-turn map, registries) behind request-id-keyed shared state so a crash, OOM or replica move does not lose in-flight cancels and queued messages. Graceful redeploys are already covered by drain, so this is scale-readiness hardening rather than a precondition for durable runs.

**Reference practice.** Idempotent command receipts are a differentiator (3 of 12); queue and steer controls are common (5 of 12). No reference solves multi-replica run control: T3 Code routes a CommandEnvelope through a single-process in-memory queue and persists only receipts and events in SQL [SRC], and DeepSeek documents that its contract is in-process [DOC]. Claude's stream recovery as observed is poll-based, with fast polls, a slow-poll phase and an exhausted state; its queue operations are local desktop IPC, not shared server state [SRC]. The priority therefore comes from Verevon's own code.

**Verevon today and approach.** Cancel flags (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/cancel_registry.rs:1-40), queued input (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/queued_input.rs:1-80) and the duplicate-send cache (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/idempotency_registry.rs:1-40) are per-process memory with in-code promotion notes. The Redis stream buffer is already set in the shipped compose block (apps/Model Plane/deploy/docker-compose.yml:226-241), a 120 s drain exists, and session-core enforces a unique (org_id, idempotency_key) on events. Only an enqueue route and a read-only queue strip exist. Scope the work to cancel and enqueue routed through a request_id-keyed NATS subject, as the in-code notes propose, and reuse session-core idempotency for durable receipts. Drop the editable queue and split send button unless the owner overrides the documented ruling that the model classifies mid-run input at the round boundary.

**Dependencies and risks.** Redis/Dragonfly in every deploy (cache-mode at 256 MB can evict) and a defined down-behaviour; no model-gateway replicas are configured today; ZDR turns stay memory-only.

## 6. Nice to have

Nice-to-have items are worth recording but not scheduling. All thirty sit in Wave 5 (W5): most depend on Priority work such as the sandbox computer ([F-t3-009](#F-t3-009)), the durable capability binding ([F-t3-003](#F-t3-003)) or the server message tree ([F-t3-012](#F-t3-012)), several restate a slice of an existing Verevon plan, and a few are unique to one reference with documentation-only evidence. Each entry gives the verified status, the best reference practice and the adjusted approach in one line; Zero-Data-Retention (ZDR) constraints are noted where they bind.

### 6.1 Conversation, review and workspace operations

- <a id="F-t3-069"></a>**F-t3-069 · Rewind with undo preview, read-only side chat and spin-off tasks, built on the message tree** (Planned · M · Model Plane with Frontend Plane · W5). Side chat is an ephemeral child thread seeded with a read-only snapshot of the parent context so a user can ask "why did it do X?" mid-run; spin-off proposes a draft thread chip. Rewind is already planned (I10, I11, OD19) and the rest waits on [F-t3-012](#F-t3-012). Reference: Claude desktop and Codex, whose ephemeral read-only fork is the safest model [SRC]; side chat appears in 6 of 12 references. Verevon messages are a linear table with no parent pointer (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/session-core/migrations/0001_init.sql:14-24); build side chat as an unpersisted model-gateway invocation with an explicit read-only tool allowlist, never persisted under ZDR.

- <a id="F-t3-076"></a>**F-t3-076 · Changed-files card per turn and a review gate before workspace promotion (accept or reject by file or hunk, with feedback to the agent)** (Partial · L · Model Plane with Frontend Plane · W5). A per-turn diff card and a hold on the overlay until the owner accepts or rejects paths, with reasons sent back as steering. Reference: T3 Code changed-files section with aggregated stats [SRC]; Antigravity accept or reject with FILE, HUNK and ALL scope and written feedback returned to the trajectory [SRC]; 5 of 9 coding references gate promotion. Verevon's diff_and_upload yields ChangedFile with base_hash but runs once at lease release and nothing calls PromoteWorkspace (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/execution-core/src/workspace_hydrate.rs:102-125); decide who promotes first, then add sandbox-manager RPCs to list, discard and promote selected paths. Follows [F-t3-030](#F-t3-030).

- <a id="F-t3-080"></a>**F-t3-080 · Library of generated and uploaded files across chats, with org publishing built on Data Plane documents** (Partial · M · Data Plane v2 with Frontend and Control Planes · W5). Register generated files and promoted artifacts (non-ZDR) as Data Plane documents carrying their origin thread; the library is a filtered document view and "publish" means "save as document, then share through resource_grants". Reference: Manus library [SRC]; Claude desktop private-by-default published artifacts with roles [SRC]; 3 of 12 references each. Verevon's Reports and Findings pages read the local transcript cache with a title regex (apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/src/features/chat/components/ChatUtilityPage.tsx:49-70) while document create, listing and ShareDialog already exist; add a chat_artifact type on the knowledge document route, text-only, with a ZDR gate and no public links by default.

- <a id="F-t3-086"></a>**F-t3-086 · Review as a run: read-only reviewer subagent with strict P0-P3 findings, merged with the deterministic source checks** (Absent · M · Model Plane with Frontend Plane · W5). A "review this" run over an artifact revision, turn diff or attachments returns validated, located findings and may report nothing; source checks merge into the same card. Reference: Codex review/start with four targets, strict findings JSON and a "report only what the author would fix" standard [SRC]; Claude desktop findings card with apply fix [SRC]; 4 of 9 coding references. Verevon's only reviewer is source_validation.rs, a forced review_tool call that gates artifact drafting (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/source_validation.rs:1394-1410); build the first slice on the inline chat path with structured_output_schema and server-side validation, keeping model findings and source checks visibly distinct.

- <a id="F-t3-096"></a>**F-t3-096 · Operability long tail: per-thread run export, report-a-problem and per-run support bundles, SPA long-task telemetry, a product-guide skill and a public event API** (Partial · M · Model Plane with Frontend and Control Planes · W5). Small additions for support and enterprise integration; none blocks the vision. Reference: Claude desktop diagnostic bundle with per-section consent and a correlation id shown to the user [SRC]; Antigravity debug bundle and guide skill [SRC]; support bundles 6 of 12 references, mostly desktop tools. Verevon's "Give feedback" dialog files an Inbox conversation with only the page URL (apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/src/features/core/components/FeedbackWidget.tsx:83), the Run Console proof bundle has no download or trace id, and per-answer export plus run-event SSE with an OpenAPI spec exist; extend these, keeping every bundle content-free on ZDR and temporary runs.

### 6.2 Model Plane tooling, orchestration and routing

- <a id="F-t3-070"></a>**F-t3-070 · Tool efficiency at scale: a deferred tool index, typed tool outputs, code mode through a gated sandbox bridge, background jobs and WebMCP page tools** (Partial · XL · Model Plane · W5). One deferred index over built-ins, provider actions and Model Context Protocol (MCP) tools, optional typed outputs, then code mode where sandboxed code calls tools as async functions with every call re-entering capability-core EvaluatePolicy. Reference: Hermes tool_search bridge with core tools never deferred [SRC]; Codex code mode in a V8 isolate with DeepSeek's rule that each sub-call re-enters the guarded pipeline [SRC]; tool search 4 of 12 references. Verevon stages mcp_catalog and mcp_call definitions but tool_loop.rs refuses every mcp__ call (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/tool_loop.rs:625-645), so [F-t3-003](#F-t3-003) and [F-t3-009](#F-t3-009) come first and the index must not break the purpose-lock allowlist.

- <a id="F-t3-071"></a>**F-t3-071 · Coordinator-mode orchestration on Temporal with typed child results, a fleet panel and gated fan-out** (Partial · M · Model Plane with Frontend Plane · W5). A coordinator run with no effect tools, typed child results, a visible and stoppable fleet panel, and a confirmation above a fan-out threshold. Reference: Cursor Project mode with a non-editing orchestrator and single-writer progress record [SRC]; Manus Workflow Composer confirming above about 20 agent calls [SRC]; T3 Code task rows and fleet banner [SRC]; orchestration patterns 6 of 12 references. Verevon's TeamCreate and TeamList are an in-memory DashMap with no caller (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/model-gateway/src/coordinator.rs:1-25), but subagent.task and research_fan_out already give budgeted fan-out with lineage; extend those with a validated per-item output contract instead of rebuilding on Temporal, and mark the Temporal workflows ZDR-denying first.

- <a id="F-t3-072"></a>**F-t3-072 · Resilient routing: failure vocabulary, credential pools, benching, usage-limit and refusal fallbacks, provider health** (Partial · M · Model Plane · W5). A closed failure enum (auth, quota, rate limit, context too long, server, timeout, refusal, empty), per-provider-and-model cooldown and benching until reset_at, and an availability field on ModelInfo. Reference: Hermes failover and credential pools with cooldowns following the provider-declared reset [SRC]; T3 Code provider health snapshot [SRC]; 2 to 3 of 12 references per facet. Verevon's FallbackChain already walks providers with ZDR, privacy and residency gates before every hop (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/inference-core/src/provider/fallback.rs:75-110) but has no cooldown or benching; drop automatic usage-limit fallback on the subscription route because it clashes with ownership rule 17 and the no-failover decision.

- <a id="F-t3-073"></a>**F-t3-073 · Per-step routing decision and model continuity: resolver, lifecycle successors and a reasoning replay envelope** (Partial · L · Model Plane with Session Core · W5). Extend intent::Decision with reason codes, carry it on USAGE_ENVELOPE and assistant-message metadata, show model and reason in the Details popover; add one resolve_model() with fixed precedence, lifecycle successors, and a replay envelope never persisted on ZDR turns. Reference: Hermes reasoning replay and single resolver [SRC]; Codex lifecycle upgrade target [SRC]; replay 4 of 12 references. Verevon resolves a Decision per request but fallback.rs only logs it (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/inference-core/src/provider/fallback.rs:1011-1045); the step-level RoutingDecision is already planned in MODEL_PLANE_IMPROVEMENTS_2026.md section 9.

- <a id="F-t3-092"></a>**F-t3-092 · Model Council: parallel multi-model answers with a disagreement synthesis** (Absent · M · Model Plane · W5). Fan one question out to N catalogue models, then a synthesiser lists agreement, disagreement and unique claims side by side, with the cost shown first. Distinctive but N-fold in cost. Reference: Perplexity Model Council, 1 of 11 references, documentation only [DOC]. Verevon has no multi-model fan-out: research_fan_out runs N items on one model, and the Agents page "Compare AI models" button is disabled with a comment that no backend exists (apps/Frontend Plane/verevonv3/src/features/agents/components/ChatbotPlayground.tsx:90-125). Place it in model-gateway as a tool-free, member-capped run; every member passes the same ZDR, privacy and residency gates and a subscription-route member is opt-in. Needs the [F-t3-028](#F-t3-028) cost card.

- <a id="F-t3-095"></a>**F-t3-095 · Second-order latency and cache levers: speed tier, prewarm, cache-preserving in-band changes, Responses continuation** (Partial · L · Model Plane · W5). Split the speed tier from reasoning effort (declared per model record, cost multiplier in cost-core), prewarm on composer focus only if turn_timing proves a gain, append-only in-band updates, and Responses continuation on platform routes. Reference: Codex service tiers and Responses WebSocket [SRC]; Cursor prewarm and progressive hydration [SRC]; DeepSeek harness in-history updates [SRC]; speed tier 3 of 12 references. Verevon's Quick mode already maps to the priority tier, but only on the subscription route and conflated with a 1024-token thinking budget (apps/Model Plane/rust/services/inference-core/src/provider/codex_subscription.rs:130-200); restrict provider-held continuation state to non-ZDR turns and gate each lever with the latency_levers.rs kill switch.

### 6.3 Policy, privacy, flags and admission

- <a id="F-t3-074"></a>**F-t3-074 · Editable permission rules, CEL policy and an MCP tool gate with consent receipts** (Partial · L · Model Plane · W5). A durable rule store over typed targets (domain, recipient, command prefix) resolved strictest-wins so "don't ask again" shows the exact rule; optional Common Expression Language (CEL) rules and an MCP gate with single-use receipts later. Reference: Antigravity action(target) grant store with deny over ask over allow [SRC]; Claude strictness lattice, CEL rules and MCP consent receipts [SRC]; declarative rules 2 of 12 references.
